### Page 2

世界経済：AI・防衛関連需要を受け景況感は好調。一方でインフレ圧力の広がりも確認
 9月のグローバル製造業PMIは53.0（8月52.3）と堅調。非製造業は54.5（8月53.6）と40か月ぶりの高水準
― 製造業はAI・防衛関連投資需要を受け生産、新規受注ともに上昇。非製造業も幅広い業種で業況が改善
― 一方、仕入・販売価格指数はともに上昇。エネルギーや輸送価格上昇によるインフレ圧力の広がりを示唆
 国・地域別では米国・ユーロ圏が製造業・非製造業ともに改善。とりわけ米国の好調が目立つ
― 米国ではAI関連支出の拡大に伴い機械・設備投資および生産が増加。非製造業においても情報通信業が突出して好調
― ユーロ圏でも、AI投資に支えられたIT関連サービスが特に力強く拡大。専門・商業サービスも改善
1
製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数） 非製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数）
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数：企業に対するアン
ケート調査結果から作成した指数。新規受注や生産高、雇用、出荷遅延などの指数

に一定のウエイトを掛けて算出。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点
（出所）S&P Global、中国国家統計局より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数（非製造業）：企業に対
するアンケート調査結果から作成した指数。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点

### Page 3

金融市場概況：投資家心理が悪化し、日米とも株安・金利低下
 AIインフラ投資への信用不安の高まりから、日米株価はハイテク株を中心に下落
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機に。原油高に伴うインフレ懸念も下押し圧力に
― 投資家心理が悪化するなかで、安全資産とされる日米国債に資金が流入、前月までの金利上昇基調は一巡
 ドル円は、1ドル＝150円台後半で方向感の乏しい展開
― 日米金利とも低下し、日米金利差は概ね横ばい圏で推移
― 原油高が輸入増を通じて円安要因となる一方、日米株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化に伴うユーロ安は円高要因に
2

米国：インフレ率は高止まり、油価再加速で当面は物価高が続く懸念
 原油高や関税の影響から物価は加速
―9月のコアCPIは前月比＋0.3％（年率＋4.1％）と8月（前月比＋0.3％、年率＋3.5％）から伸びが拡大
― コアサービスでは住居費や輸送サービスなどが押し上げに寄与。コア財については、衣料品や玩具などが上昇。関税引
き上げに伴う価格転嫁が続いている模様
 足元で原油価格が再加速していることを踏まえれば、当面は物価の上振れリスクが残存する懸念
― シカゴ連銀が公表する週次の小売物価指標は9月後半から10月初めにかけて上昇しており、物価の高止まりを示唆
3
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
項目別コアCPI 週次小売物価（シカゴ連銀）

（出所） シカゴ連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：足元の消費はガソリン高や関税に伴う物価上昇で足踏みも、年末商戦は堅調な見通し
 消費は足踏み。ガソリン価格高騰や関税に伴う物価高が家計の購買力を下押し
―9月の実質小売売上高は前月比▲0.5％と減少。ガソリン小売が減少したほか、自動車や衣料品も低調に推移
 物価高は逆風も、2026年の年末商戦は堅調予想
― 米マスターカードは、実質小売売上高（除くガソリン・自動車）は前年比＋2.7％と、前年（同＋2.1％）から伸びが拡大する
と予想。雇用環境の改善などを背景に幅広い所得層で安定した消費がみられると指摘
4
（注） ガソリンはCPIを用いて実質化。その他は、8、9月のみ各品目に対応するCPIを用

米国：雇用は減速も、AI関連需要が下支え
 9月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋2.9万人と市場予想（同＋9.0万人）を下回る結果。7月・8月も下方修正
― 民間部門が前月差＋4.6万人（8月同＋8.9万人）と増勢が鈍化したほか、政府部門が同▲1.7万人と再びマイナス転化
― 民間部門では、旺盛なAI投資需要などを背景に、製造や建設、建築・エンジニアリングなどが増加基調を維持したもの
の、情報、金融、専門サービスなどの高賃金セクターが減少
― 時間当たり賃金の伸びは前月比＋0.1％（8月同＋0.3％）と鈍化。こちらも市場予想（同＋0.3％）を下回る結果
― 総じて冴えない結果ながら、3カ月平均では＋5万人程度を維持しており、雇用は減速しつつも失速には至らず

5
（出所） 米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 7

米国：対人型業種の雇用に底堅さ。労働参加率上昇で失業率は小幅上昇
 対人接触度の高い業種では雇用に底堅さ、AIによる代替が進みにくい可能性
― ヘルスケアや建設、飲食サービスなど、対人接触機会の多い業種では雇用が増加する一方、経理・会計サービス、ITシ
ステム開発などでは低調
 失業率は4.2％と小幅悪化も引き続き低水準を維持
― 主に16-24歳の若年層およびプライムエイジ層の労働参加率の上昇が押し上げに寄与した模様
6
（注）1.バブルの大きさは各産業の雇用者数
2. 対人接触度は、O*NETの職業別のPhysical Proximity（業務上、他者と物理的に近
接する度合い）を、各産業の職業別雇用者構成で加重平均した指数
3.コロナ禍からの雇用回復の影響を除くため、雇用者数の変化率は2023年末を起点
に算出
（出所） 米労働省、セントルイス連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：生産は底堅く推移。AI・防衛が投資財生産をけん引、半導体増産も寄与
 2026年8月の鉱工業生産は前年比＋1.4％と底堅い結果
― 半導体関連の生産が引き続きけん引。生産能力が2022年対比1.7倍に拡大するなど生産体制の増強が背景。事実、主
要半導体メーカーによる工場新設・増強を受け、MOSロジック（GPU、CPUなど演算用）の量産が本格化
 AI・防衛分野を中心に投資財生産も堅調さを維持
― 投資関連財はAI・防衛が押し上げる形で同＋4.7％と増勢継続。国防・宇宙関連設備は同＋6.1％ と全体を上回る伸び。
地政学リスクの高まりを背景に軍用機やミサイル・防空システムなどの生産体制強化が図られている模様
7

生活費
期待より悪い 期待通り 期待より良い
（シェア）
米国：中間選挙を目前に住宅市場は軟調、アフォーダビリティへの不満が共和党への逆風に
 住宅ローン金利は足元で再び上昇
― 借入コストの負担増により、足元の住宅ローン申請件数は減少
 物価上昇が生活コストを押し上げるなか、金利上昇による住宅取得環境の悪化も家計の不満を助長する要因に
― トランプ大統領の生活費への政策対応は約7割が「期待より悪い」と評価しており、支持率は低下基調
―11月に控える中間選挙では、アフォーダビリティへの対応が引き続き主要な論点

8

欧州：G7は原油・ディーゼルの協調放出で合意。米国の輸出制限リスクは後退
9
 G7はIEAを通じ4カ月で1億バレルの石油備蓄放出に合意。初期20日間はディーゼルを前倒し放出
― 背景にディーゼル価格高騰。中間選挙を控えたトランプ大統領は、禁輸も辞さない姿勢を強調（米国は世界需要の4.9％
を供給）。米国からの輸出が欧州の需要を支える一方、米国よりも潤沢な在庫を保有する欧州に圧力をかけた格好
― 欧州は石油製品消費に占めるディーゼル比率が高く、価格高騰を回避する思惑も協調放出の合意を後押し
― 合意ではG7域内でのエネルギー輸出制限回避も確認され、米国によるEU向け禁輸リスクはひとまず後退
石油製品の製品別消費割合の国際比較
（出所）Energy Instituteより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 11

欧州：ディーゼル需給逼迫は残存。欧州のインフレ上振れ要因に
10
 ロシアのディーゼル輸出は足元で急減。中東からの輸出減、中国の輸出停止も重なり、世界需要の約6％が失われた格好
―G7の1億バレル放出で当面の供給不安は緩和するものの、構造的な供給不足の解消には至らず
―IEAも、中東からの原油輸出は回復する一方、石油製品の供給制約はなお深刻と指摘。ディーゼル需給の逼迫が続け
ば、輸送・物流コストを通じたインフレ圧力が残存
 欧州では9月HICPが再加速。ディーゼル高が輸送・物流コストを通じて追加的なインフレ上振れ要因となるリスク
―9月消費者物価（HICP）は前年比＋3.8％（前月：同＋3.2％）と伸び加速。エネルギー価格の上昇が主因
今般の備蓄放出量と停止中のディーゼル輸出
（注） 世界需要およびロシア・中国の輸出量は2025年実績。中東は2026年8月実績を参
考に、輸出量を前年の4分の1としてシェアを概算
（出所）EIA、IEA、OPEC、各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（出所）Eurostatより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：EU・中国はハイブリッド車の輸入制限で暫定合意。同様の措置は他の品目に広がる可能性
11
 EUと中国は、貿易不均衡の是正措置について暫定合意。中国からのハイブリッド車輸入は従来見通しの半分以下に
―EUが中国産EVに追加関税を賦課した2024年以降、追加関税の対象とならないハイブリッド車輸入が急増
― 欧州委員会の試算では、今回の措置によって2030年までの4年間で数百万台の輸入を削減見込み。直近では、EUが輸
入するハイブリッド車の約半分を中国車が占めており、域内自動車市場に一定のインパクト
 欧州委員会は、幅広い産業で貿易不均衡を是正する必要性を示唆。同様の措置が他の品目にも広がる可能性
― 欧州委員会のモニタリング対象（輸入増加と価格下落が併存する品目）は、中国からの輸入品が最多

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
12

欧州：長期金利上昇を受け、一部ECB高官は追加利上げの必要性低下を指摘
13
 フランスでは国内政治・財政面の不安から長期金利が上昇。イタリア・ギリシャなど周辺の高債務国にも波及
― フランス国債の対独利回り差は急拡大し、一時欧州債務危機（2012年）以来の水準に。その後、大統領選の有力候補と
目されるルペン氏（国民連合）が財政赤字を縮小する意欲を示し、拡大はひとまず一服
―ECBによる介入（国債買い入れ等）の可能性については、複数の高官が検討段階にないとの見方を示す
 長期金利の上昇は需要を抑制。ECB高官からは追加利上げの必要性が低下するとの見方も
― エネルギー高によるインフレ圧力と金利上昇による景気下押し圧力が併存。ECBの政策判断は一段と難しくなる可能性

中国：7～9月期の実質GDPは＋4.5％。インフラ投資の加速で、通年の成長率目標を確保へ
 7～9月期の実質GDP成長率は前年比＋4.5％。1～9月の累計で同＋4.6％と、通年目標「＋4.5～5.0％」の範囲内
―7～9月期は、純輸出が＋1.8％Ptと成長をけん引する一方、総資本形成は＋0.6％Ptまで縮小。内需の弱さ鮮明
― 中国政府は地方政府専項債の発行枠を追加（2,500億元）。インフラ投資を加速させ、成長率目標の達成を確保へ

― 投資は同▲7.2％（前月：同▲10.6％） 。前年の反動増は限定的で、インフラ投資は同▲10.5％と2ケタ減が続く

中国：政府は住宅ローンの利子補給で市場テコ入れ。人民元相場は緩やかな元高が継続
 中国政府が9/28、新規住宅ローン金利の年率1％分を補助する政策を発表。低迷が続く不動産市場をテコ入れ
― 対象物件の購入金額を150万元（3,500万円）以下に限定し、低中所得者層の住宅ローン負担を軽減する
― これにより、余剰在庫に苦しむ地方都市での実需を掘り起こし、市況のさらなる悪化に歯止めをかけたい考え
 人民元相場は、中国の貿易黒字が過去最高水準を記録する中、緩やかに元高が進行し1ドル＝6.6元台に突入
― 高水準の貿易黒字を背景とした人民元買いの実需が強いことから、当面は緩やかな元高が続く見込み
― ただ、米FRBの利上げで米中金利差が拡大しており、さらなる拡大が意識されれば元安に転じる可能性も

日本：旺盛なAI関連需要が製造業の景況感を押し上げ。利益計画は大幅上方修正
16
 大企業の業況判断は製造業が6四半期連続の改善。非製造業は悪化も一時的な悪天候が主因で変調の懸念は低い
― 業種別では、化学、石油・石炭製品、生産用機械等が改善。旺盛なAI関連需要や原油の供給不足懸念の後退等が背景

― とりわけ大企業・製造業では、輸出向け売上高の上方修正が顕著。上期の実績が大幅に上振れたことを反映したもの
― 世界的なAI関連需要の好調持続が見込まれるもと、下期も堅調な輸出が続けば、利益計画は一段の上方修正余地

日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ
18
 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み
（注） コメは玄米及び精米。食品関連は、飲食料品及び農林水産物（除くコメ）

米国債券市場：インフレ加速懸念で上昇後、AI関連株の下落を受け金利は低下
 10月の米10年国債利回りは、一時5.3％台まで上昇したものの、足元では5.0％前後まで低下
― 月初は、原油高等によるインフレ加速懸念を受け、長期金利は約24年ぶりの高水準となる5.3％台まで上昇
― 中旬以降は、AI関連株の先行きに対する慎重な見方から、安全資産である米国債への需要が拡大し、長期金利は低下
 今後も、中東情勢に伴う原油価格の変動やAI関連株の動向など、金利を左右する不確実性が多く、振れの大きい展開が続
く可能性

国内債券・為替：株安を背景に長期金利は低下。ドル円は150円台後半で横ばい圏
 長期金利は、株安を背景に低下。10年金利・30年金利とも、10月上旬から30bps以上の大幅低下
― 米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格急落が株安の契機となり、安全資産とされる日本国債に資金が流入
― 先行き、株式市場の混乱が続けば、短期的には一段の金利低下余地も。もっとも、日銀は四半期に一度のペースで利上
げを続けるとみられ、混乱一巡後の円金利は、再び緩やかな上昇基調に転じる見通し
 ドル円は150円台後半で方向感に欠ける展開。先行きも、150円台を中心とした円安水準が続く見込み
― 原油高は円安要因の一方、株安に伴うリスクオフや仏情勢悪化を受けたユーロ安は円高要因。目先は両者が綱引きへ

内外株式：AIインフラ投資への信用不安の高まりから、AI・半導体関連株を中心に下落

― 企業業績への期待から、10月初の日米株は上昇。S&P500は一時最高値を更新。日経平均も一時7万円台を回復
― その後は、米新興クラウドサービスの社債価格の急落を受け、AI・半導体関連株を中心に下落。原油価格の上昇に伴

うインフレ懸念も下押し圧力に。もっとも、S&P500はヘルスケア関連株等への資金シフトが進み、底堅く推移
 AIインフラ投資に対する信用不安は残存。原油価格の上昇も重石となり、日米株は軟調な推移が続くと予想
― 金利が高水準で推移する中、AIインフラの過剰投資懸念が強まり株価を下押しするリスクには留意が必要

## Document 071: 2026年10月金融数据点评：财政资金加速下达，实体信贷尚未改善

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月金融数据点评：财政资金加速下达，实体信贷尚未改善.pdf
Report source: 中诚信国际
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CPI、PPI 双双回落，通胀压力处于可控范围--
12 月价格数据点评及2022 年展望,2022 年1 月
12 日 
CPI、PPI 双双回落，通胀压力处于可控范围--
12 月价格数据点评及2022 年展望,2022 年1 月
12 日 
8

作者: 
中诚信国际 研究院

研究员 赵皓月 
hyzhao@ccxi.com.cn

研究员 张堃 
kzhang02@ccxi.com.cn

院长 袁海霞  
hxyuan@ccxi.com.cn

金融数据的三个边际改善，2026 年 10 月
15 日

如何理解直接融资占比超 50%？2026 年 9
月15 日

如何理解本月社融数据边际改善？2026 年
8 月17 日

客观看待信贷收缩，直接间接融资分化或
成常态，2026 年7 月16 日

实体信贷需求延续疲弱，结售汇支撑M1 增
速回升，2026 年6 月15 日

直接融资占比持续提升，居民、企业信贷
负增长，2026 年5 月15 日

财政资金加速下达，实体信贷尚未改善 
——2026 年 10 月金融数据点评

事件：10 月新增社融 1.14 万亿，同比多增 3251 亿元，存量社
融增速 7.2%（前值 7.1%）；金融机构新增人民币贷款-1000 亿元，
同比少增3200 亿元，金融机构各项人民币贷款余额增速4.7%（前值
4.8%）；M1 同比增长 4.4%（前值 3.2%），M2 同比增长 7.1%（前值
7.2%）。 
直接融资对间接融资替代加快，政府债券带动社融增速边际改
善。10 月新增社融 1.14 万亿，同比多增 3251 亿元，连续两个月同
比多增；社融存量增速为 7.2%，较上月上升 0.1 个百分点。从新增
社融结构来看，直接融资延续对间接融资的替代，政府债券表现亮
眼。间接融资方面，10 月人民币贷款减少5846 亿元，显著低于去年
同期水平（-154 亿元）和季节性均值（2021-2025 年同期均值，
+3966 亿元）；三项非标合计减少240 亿元，同比少减846 亿元，值
得注意的是，10 月8000 亿元新型政策性金融工具已全部投放完毕，
本月委托贷款新增 1862 亿元，较上月增加 1058 亿元，较去年同期
（去年 5000 亿集中在 10 月投放）多增 208 亿元，但尚未带动企业
贷款同步改善。直接融资方面，10 月企业债券融资规模为 3446 亿
元、同比多增 946 亿元，低利率市场环境为企业债券融资提供了良
好条件；10 月股票融资规模为 347 亿元、同比少增 348 亿元，10 月
股票市场下行压力较大，或限制 IPO 开展，降低股票融资规模；10
月政府债券融资 1.18 万亿，低基数下同比多增 6983 亿元，并显著
高于季节性均值（2021-2025 年同期均值 7989 亿元），1-10 月新增
一般债、专项债发行进度分别较 1-9 月加快 2.5 和 7.5 个百分点，
政府托底经济力度有所增强。从占比来看，1-10 月直接融资（企业
债券+股票+政府债券）占比为 52.99%，与间接融资（人民币信贷+
三项非标）占比之间的差值自今年 7 月起转正并持续走扩，直接融
资对间接融资的替代正在加快。  
金融数据点评 2026 年11 月16 日  2026 年第54 期

如需订阅研究报告，敬请联系 
中诚信国际品牌与投资人服务部 
赵 耿 010-66428731 
gzhao@ccxi.com.cn

### Page 2

1 
www.ccxi.com.cn 
10 月金融数据点评： 
财政资金加速下达，实体信贷尚未改善 
居民信贷延续疲弱态势，票据融资拖累企业信贷负增长。10 月金融机构新增人民币贷款
-1000 亿元，同比少增 3200 亿元。从结构来看，居民部门新增短期、中长期信贷均为负。10
月居民部门人民币贷款减少4300 亿元，同比多减696 亿元，其中，短期贷款减少2700 亿元，
同比少减 166 亿元，居民消费贷和经营贷均有小幅改善，但在居民收入疲弱、汽车消费低迷、
经营贷利率上调且不良率攀升等因素影响下，居民新增短期贷款能否延续改善仍有待观察。
10 月居民部门中长期贷款减少 1563 亿元，同比多减 863 亿元，10 月 30 大中城市商品房成交
面积同比下降 18.14%，自 8 月以来跌幅持续走扩，拖累居民中长期信贷表现，同时也表明 9
月末房贷贴息政策效果有限。企业新增短期、中长期信贷均为正，但票据融资拖累较大。10
月企业部门新增人民币贷款-1600 亿元，同比少增5100 亿元，其中，短期贷款增加100 亿元、
同比多增 2000 亿元，中长期贷款增加 1600 亿元、同比多增 1300 亿元，但这主要源于基数较
低，与2021-2025 年同期均值相比，本月企业中长期信贷少增928 亿元。10 月新增票据融资-
3117 亿元，同比少增 8123 亿元，拖累本月企业信贷表现，主要原因在于，其一，9 月国务院
发文《关于加强中小企业回款难问题治理有关工作的通知》指出“压降商业汇票和电子凭证
业务规模”，企业开票规模下降；其二，“反内卷”持续进行下银行规模情结或有所淡化，
以票充贷必要性下降；其三，去年同期基数较高。

10 月金融数据点评： 
财政资金加速下达，实体信贷尚未改善 
M1 增速上行、M2 增速下滑，M2-M1 剪刀差收窄。10 月 M1 同比增长 4.4%，较上月上升
1.2 个百分点，由于10 月个人和企业新增活期存款同比改善幅度较小、新增非银行支付机构
客户备付金同比走弱，因此合理推断 10 月 M1 增速的改善主要是由机关团体活期存款同比大
幅改善驱动，随着 10 月政府债券发行提速，以及 8000 亿元新型政策性金融工具落地，财政
资金密集拨付，但尚未形成实际支出，导致 10 月机关团体活期存款显著改善，带动本月 M1
增速改善。此外，去年 M1 基数较低也是本月 M1 增速抬升的重要原因，去年 10 月 M1 余额减
少了 1.15 万亿，为同期有数据以来的最低值。10 月 M2 同比增长 7.1%，较上月下降 0.1 个
百分点。一方面，10 月新增人民币贷款为负，影响货币派生；另一方面，10 月政府债券发

行规模较大，企业或个人认购政府债券也压低 M2 增速。10 月 M2-M1 剪刀差为 2.7%，较上月
收窄1.3 个百分点。 
10 月人民币升值有所提速，下半月汇率与中间价偏离走扩。10 月人民币在岸汇率在
6.6557 至 6.7014 间波动，离岸汇率在 6.7171 至 6.7144 间波动，人民币兑美元汇率延续走
强。10 月人民币汇率较上月升值 0.74%，为 6 月以来的最高涨幅，人民币升值加速。此外，
10 月下半月起人民币汇率与中间价偏离走扩，上半月人民币汇率与中间价偏离均值为 182BP，
下半月走扩 360BP 至 542BP，人民币市场汇率表现较为强势。10 月人民币加速升值的主要原
因：一方面，10 月美联储、欧洲央行、日本央行均宣布维持政策利率（区间）不变，加息暂
停；另一方面，10 月我国贸易顺差同比增长 19.64%，企业结汇需求依然较大，且人民币升
值预期进一步推动企业结汇意愿上升，形成了人民币汇率的自我强化。

10 月金融数据点评： 
财政资金加速下达，实体信贷尚未改善 
整体来看，10 月金融数据表明政策发力增强，但尚未完全传导至实体经济。10 月政府
债券和委托贷款均同比多增，并显著高于季节性均值，政策发力迹象较为明显。但从政策效
果来看，10 月企业中长期贷款虽然有所改善，但基数因素比较明显，同时，10 月机关团体
活期存款同比高增，表明虽然财政发力，但资金尚未完全进入实体经济，实体融资需求的改
善并不显著。展望后续，从政策发力到传导至实体融资需求可能因为项目储备不足、冬季施
工受限等原因存在时滞，11、12 月企业融资需求或有所改善但幅度可能不大，可关注委托贷
款和企业中长期信贷表现。此外，结合 10 月房地产销售数据和居民中长期贷款数据来看，9
月末的房贷贴息政策效果尚未充分显现，后续可持续关注以上指标，同时建议视情况扩大贴
息范围。

10 月金融数据点评：
财政资金加速下达，实体信贷尚未改善
汇率方面，10 月人民币升值加速，后续或延续升值趋势、但速度放缓。一方面，11 月3
日，中国人民银行宣布自 2026 年 11 月 15 日起，上调金融机构外汇存款准备金率 2 个百分
点至6%，虽然不能扭转人民币汇率走势，但能够缓解人民币短期升值过快的压力，并向市场
释放稳汇率信号。另一方面，市场普遍预测 12 月美国还有一次加息，美元指数或进一步走
强，在一定程度上压低人民币汇率。但需要注意的是，美国中期选举结束，民主党重掌参议
院和众议院，或将缓和甚至结束中东冲突，国际油价有望下行，或降低美联储 12 月加息概
率、导致美元走弱，同时，国内年末结汇需求季节性抬升，均是人民币走强的支撑因素。因
此整体来看，年末人民币汇率仍将延续升值趋势，但升值速度或将放缓。

鴻海 (2317 TT) 
規模效益顯現

永豐觀點 
AI 伺服器為成長主軸，規模效益顯現，觀察政策長期對 AI 資料中心建置的影響。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持鴻海買進建議：鴻海 3Q26 財報、4Q26 展望優於預期。2027 年維持
以 AI 伺服器為成長主軸，公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求仍大於供給，關鍵
零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業 AI 資料中心建置
時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同時體現獲利率效
益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。研究部預估鴻海 2026 年/2027 年稅後
EPS 19.33 元/25.51 元。維持鴻海買進建議，目標價 356 元(14x2027FEPS)。

ESG 評析 
鴻海企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。

### Page 2

2

鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產
品，以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯
(601138 SH)、富智康(2038 HK)及鴻騰精密(6088 HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研
發資源在電動車、數位醫療、機器人、AI、半導體、5G 等領域。主要客戶為 Apple、
HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、北美四大 CSP 等。3Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
29%、雲端網路 54%、電腦終端 12%、元件及其他 5%。 
3Q26 財報優於預期：鴻海 3Q26 毛利率 6.02%，營益率 3.83%，優於預估的
5.93%、3.77%。鴻海營收規模放大，營運槓桿效益顯現。業外收入貢獻 33 億元，
稅後淨利 775 億元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元，優於預估 5.10 元。3Q26 鴻海存貨金額
1.5 兆，季增 10%，年增 27%，存貨天數 45.7 天，季減 3 天，年減 4 天。存貨狀
況尚屬健康。 
4Q26 展望略優於預期：鴻海展望 4Q26 營運，AI 伺服器出貨量季增高雙位數(部
分專案採 Consignment 商業模式)，預期 ASIC 及 GPU 出貨續增，Vera Rubin AI
伺服器機櫃單價跳升，推估雲端網路營收季增約 15%。消費性產品 iPhone 維持
強勁出貨動能，PC 需求則持續疲弱季減。研究部略為上修 4Q26 營收預估季增
17%，稅後 EPS 上修至 5.99 元。 
AI 伺服器為鴻海營運增長動能 留意長期資料中心建置影響性：因應客戶 AI 建置
需求，鴻海持續支出投入產能，主要客戶為美系大型 CSP、AI 模型商及 Neocloud，
降低資金及庫存風險。並透過與客戶改成採用 Consignment 商業模式，提高營運
資金效率，並優化獲利率表現。研究部預期鴻海維持 Vera Rubin 的主要供應商角
色，整體市占率維持達 40%以上，同時鴻海 2027 年將增加美系 CSP AI ASIC 伺
服器機櫃供應，為 2027 年營運增長動能。公司表示 2027 年 AI 伺服器產業需求
仍大於供給，關鍵零組件持續吃緊，惟仍需觀察期中選舉後，政策長期對大型企業
AI 資料中心建置時程的變化。研究部認為鴻海在提高市占率、營收規模放大的同
時體現獲利率效益、獲利金額擴大以及營運資金運用效率。 
綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收 11.2 兆，年增 38%，毛利率 6.0%，營益率
3.7%，稅後淨利 2,710 億，年增 43%，稅後 EPS 19.33 元。2027 年營收 15.5 兆
元，年增 38%，毛利率 5.4%，營益率 3.4%，稅後淨利 3,562 億元，年增 31%，
稅後 EPS 25.51 元。

## Document 122: 261112_citi_msi

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_citi_msi.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins from ASP Pass-Through and Richer Mix
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), 
beating Citi/cons by 27%/45% on stronger-than-expected margins and 
non-op gains. GPM of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8 ppts YoY) topped Citi/cons 
at 15.8%/14.5%, as ASP hikes passed through rising component costs while 
moving-avg inventory costs lagged, alongside a richer high-end product 
mix. For 4Q26E, we expect further ASP increases and a rising server 
contribution to largely offset seasonal shipment declines. Into 2027, we 
expect PC demand to stay weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases moderately. We raise 2026E-28E earnings and our 
TP to NT$145 (10x 2027E EPS). We stay Neutral as the share price already 
reflects the pass-through upside.
3Q26: Margin-led beat on cost pass-through and better mix — MSI reported 
3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), beating Citi/cons forecasts 
by 27%/45%, mainly on stronger-than-expected margins and non-op gains. GPM 
of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8ppts YoY) came in above Citi/cons at 15.8%/14.5%. 
Mgmt. attributed the upside to 1) ASP increase, as MSI raised prices in line with 
suppliers’ cost increases while its moving-average inventory costs rose less; and 2) 
a higher mix of high-end products. Non-op gains, including FX gains of over 
NT$100mn, further boosted the bottom line.
Price hikes to continue; 2027 PC market to keep shrinking — We reiterate our view 
that 4Q26 shipments will decline seasonally from the 3Q26 peak. However, mgmt. 
expects rising raw material costs and continued pass-through to keep lifting ASPs, 
offsetting lower volumes and making the seasonal decline milder than in prior years. 
The server business should also contribute more, potentially reaching a high-single-
digit revenue share. Into 2027, we expect weak PC demand amid elevated costs, 
with MSI’s MB and PC shipments declining by over 10%. Pass-through room should 
narrow as memory price increases moderately, though margins should stay elevated 
for a while, as mgmt. expects memory supply to stay tight in the coming quarters.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 6-27%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through and higher margin assumptions. 
We adjust TP to NT$145 (from NT$140), implying 10x 2027E EPS (from 12x). We 
believe the share price already reflects MSI’s opportunity to pass component costs 
through to end products, and we expect limited upside going forward, given that the 
PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain. Maintain Neutral.

### Page 6

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
13 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$145 is based on 10x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 129: 261112_ml_kingslide

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_ml_kingslide.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

King Slide

Buy: strong 3Q26 results, 88.2% GM; solid 
’27/28E sales/margin; cut PO to NT$12,988

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 12,988 TWD | Price: 8,165 TWD

3Q26 results: another impressive beat with 88.2% GM 
King Slide’s 3Q26 GM/OPM hit record highs of 88.2%/82.9% for the fourth/second 
consecutive quarter, respectively, well above BofAe of 83.7%/78.2% and consensus of 
84.0%/78.6%. OP (+62% QoQ, +383% YoY) was 7%/5% above BofAe/consensus. We 
attribute the solid margins/results to sizable sales scale (+60% QoQ, +297% YoY) and 
better operating efficiency. With non-operating gain (mainly net interest income), NP 
(+65% QoQ, +266% YoY) was 8%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3). 
BofAe 4Q26 sales -20% QoQ; solid 2027/28 sales/margin 
We now expect King Slide’s 4Q26 sales to decline 20% QoQ, from the high 3Q26 base 
and amid GPU platform transition. We believe Sep/Oct sales (-31%/+2% MoM) shall 
serve as evidence for slower order momentum. Our 4Q26 sales forecast implies flat-to-
slightly higher MoM sales in Nov/Dec. We now expect another 45%/20% YoY sales 
growth in 2027/28, with stronger pull-in demand resuming in 1Q27. In our view, 
2027/28 sales should be supported by various AI projects across platforms (GPU/ASIC/ 
AMD) and clients (global/regional CSPs, neoclouds, AI start-ups, enterprises). We also 
keep monitoring the capacity ramp-up from the new US Houston site, which may bring 
further sales upside. We expect high and sustainable GM at 85.0% both in 2027/28. We 
see FX as a potential swing factor to GM, given the company’s natural-hedging policy.  
Trim PO to NT$12,988: lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lower our 2026-27E EPS by 0-4% but lift our 2028E EPS by 7%, mainly to reflect 
3Q26 beat and stronger margins. Yet, we cut PO to NT$12,988 (28x 2027E EPS) from 
NT$14,888 (32x 2027E EPS), with valuation base unchanged but a lower P/E multiple. 
We now use 28x (2SD above average since IPO) on valuation de-rating across AI supply 
chain amid macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on King Slide in view of its 
lion share in AI server rail market, diversified client/project base, strong margin profile.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
King Slide is the global top server rail kit manufacturer from 
Taiwan. It also supplies slide rail kit for kitchen/furniture. Its 
key server clients include global cloud service providers and 
enterprise servers.

King Slide was founded in 1986 and was publicly listed on 
Taiwan Stock Exchange on 25 June 2008. Its headquarter 
and main production sites are located in Kaohsiung 
(Taiwan). It also has capacity in Houston (US).

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to King Slide given its leading 
position in server rail to ride on strong AI market demand 
with rising content value thanks to increasing component 
density and customization. We highlight King Slide's early 
step into server market since 2001 with proven track 
record, much larger scale, time-to-market capability, higher 
automation level, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for 
high/above-peer margins.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
King Slide (KGSLF) 
Our PO of NT$12,988 is based on 28x 2027E EPS. We use 28x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since listing) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading position in 
server rail, solid/high margin profiles, and double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. Its 
early step into server market since 2001 with proven track record, much larger scale 
than peer, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for high/above-peer margins.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) even stronger AI server demand, (2) margin upside thanks 
to larger scale and/or better efficiency, (3) better kitchen slides market demand, (4) 
faster capacity expansion, (5) favorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) for GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/components 
shortage, (2) GM pressure due to higher raw material cost (e.g. steel, aluminum), (3) 
softer kitchen slides market demand under macro uncertainty/weakness, (4) delayed 
product certification, (5) slower capacity expansion, (6) unfavorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

### Page 1

M
Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Potential to win next gen TPU projects, while other ASIC
customers remain undisclosed.
1. Major AI ASIC project (TPU) - potential to win next generation projects.
• TPU v8 shipment track, US$2.5bn revenue in 4Q26. Reiterated US$16bn
revenue in 2027, high end of guidance. As there are more customers than
chip supply, if ABF substrate supply improves, revenue can improve.
• TPU v9 EMIB-T substrate supply is also key, company is comfortable on yield

and output trajectory.
• It sees its TPU v9 project as a major TPU SKUs for the customer. MediaTek
revenue per chip is higher than it was in v8. The compute die is handled by
the customer.
• MediaTek said it is possible for the customer to use designed TPU (eg. v8t
and v9) for AI LLM customer's rack system, besides Google's internal use.
• On third generation (TPU v10), MediaTek is working with customer's internal
team, and has high confidence to win the project.

• New generation TPU compute die handled by customer. MediaTek will focus
on D2D inference, I/O die, and chip packaging technology.
• We maintain our view that Alchip is strong candidate for TPU compute die
backend design. Based on Alchip’s NRE revenue timing, we think the
compute die could be for the TPU v10.
2. Other ASIC customers - awaiting more disclosure: MediaTek still cannot
disclose the non-China CSP customer name or project details. The engagement with

China CSP is ongoing, but needs to comply with US BIS rules.
3. ASIC business financial profile - OP margin improvement: Mediatek said a major
customer wanted to drive to full COT (customer own tool), but with the chip's
complexity growing, it hopes to sustain ASIC margin in future generations. The
company reiterated its 3Q earning call comments that ASIC op. margin is 10% above
the previous average. It reiterated its op margin can improve to 20%+ in 2027 on

higher ASIC revenue contribution. We estimate ASIC op. margin at 25-30%.
4. Smartphone business - still challenging: High memory costs continue to impact
China demand. Blended ASP for MediaTek's SoC has risen on price hikes and better
product mix.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

大銀微系統(4576.TT) 
產業：電機機械 
受惠半導體設備擴充潮，2027 年營收成⻑將⾼於 20%  
 投資評價與建議 
高毛利率的奈微米定位平台營收比重已近60%，產品結構及附加價值提升帶動
毛利率提升，技術升級、集團分工與產能區域化佈局將帶動大銀微2027年營收
成⻑高於20%。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收年增77%，4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略⾼：亞洲(台灣除外)、
台灣、美洲及歐洲地區3Q26分別年增89%、101%、127%及-12%，全球半導
體供應鏈重組與在地化採購需求強勁，3Q26營收12.2億元季增19%(年增
77%)，高階定位平台出貨佔比提升及規模經濟發揮，毛利率42.4%季增
5.3pp(年增8.6pp)，規模經濟及費用控制得宜下，營業利益率24.3%季增
6.4pp(年增13.9pp)；稅前淨利3.3億元季增113%(年增255%)，EPS 2.15元，
1~3Q26EPS 4.07元；4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略高，毛利率將維持在
40%上下。 
發展策略將持續朝向⾼附加價值市場，2027年營收成⻑將⾼於20%：未來的
發展策略將持續朝向高附加價值市場邁進，包括發展精密力量控制技術（涵蓋
先進封裝Hybrid Bonding、高速固晶機等應用），大銀微將專注於系統整合
銷售，不單獨販售E1控制器，以確保軟硬體深度綁定，帶動營收與獲利持續同
步雙位數成⻑，2027年營收成⻑高於20%。

| 營運概況 
3Q26毛利率及營益率均大增

資料來源 : CMoney 
3Q26營收年增77%，毛利率及營益率均大增：亞洲(台灣除外)、台灣、美洲及歐洲地區3Q26分別年增89%、101%、
127%及-12%，全球半導體供應鏈重組與在地化採購需求強勁，3Q26營收12.2億元季增19%(年增77%)，高階定位平台
出貨佔比提升及規模經濟發揮，毛利率42.4%季增5.3pp(年增8.6pp)，規模經濟及費用控制得宜下，營業利益率24.3%
季增6.4pp(年增13.9pp)；稅前淨利3.3億元季增113%(年增255%)，EPS 2.15元，1~3Q26EPS 4.07元；毛利率提升主
要是產品結構及附加價值提升，奈微米定位平台由於高度客製化、整合空氣軸承、花崗岩平台、多軸線性馬達與高階雷
射補償演算法，且直接對接半導體⼀線客戶，毛利率達40~50%；精密運動及控制元件過去單賣馬達線圈與磁軌毛利率
未及30%，但大銀微力推「馬達＋自製E1系列驅動器＋控制器」整套搭配銷售，加上高單價晶圓移載用直驅力矩馬達出
貨放量，使元件部門毛利率亦同步墊高至30~35%。 
4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略⾼，毛利率將維持在40%上下： 4Q26營收可望與3Q26持平或略高，毛利率將維持在
40%上下，目前高階平台訂單能見度達5~6個月，交期已縮短至3個月；元件類能見度約3個月，交期約2~2.5個月，大
銀微具備85%的關鍵零組件自製率（包含線性馬達、驅動器、控制器），能快速打樣並提供高彈性交期。 
發展策略將持續朝向⾼附加價值市場，2027年營收成⻑將⾼於20%：未來的發展策略將持續朝向高附加價值市場邁進，
包括發展精密力量控制技術（涵蓋先進封裝Hybrid Bonding、高速固晶機等應用），大銀微將專注於系統整合銷售，不
單獨販售E1控制器，以確保軟硬體深度綁定，帶動營收與獲利持續同步雙位數成⻑，2027年營收成⻑高於20%；隨著AI

晶片尺寸逼近光罩極限，晶圓代工龍頭與OSAT廠加速推進FOPLP與矩陣式大面積封裝，大面積玻璃或有機基板在高速
取放、曝光與AOI檢測時，傳統滾珠螺桿會產生背隙與熱變形，必須全面改採「大跨距龍門雙驅無鐵芯線性馬達平台」
與「大型花崗岩空氣軸承系統」，大銀微在此領域具備完整大尺寸平台調校經驗，單機採用金額較傳統封裝設備提升2~3
倍；環繞式閘極與晶背供電大幅增加製程道數與光學/電子束檢測站點，歐美設備客戶為提升毛利率，持續將次系統委由
大銀微ODM，訂單能見度已直達2Q27；全球AI伺服器主板與交換器板廠在泰國大舉擴產，帶動高階雷射鑽孔機、機械
鑽孔機與成型機對大銀微線性馬達與高階驅動器的強勁拉貨；配合上銀(2049)集團佈局AI人型機器人，大銀微的高轉矩
密度無框力矩馬達與微型驅動器，具備切入機器人旋轉關節與靈巧⼿電控系統之⻑期潛力。 
CPO 相關營收訂單能見度已達 2H27：AI 資料中心算力互連由銅線轉向光學互連（800G/1.6T 光模組與 CPO），光
纖陣列（FAU）與矽光晶片波導進行主動對位（Active Alignment）時，要求定位精度必須達到奈米級且兼具極高反
應速度與抗震性，大銀微系統能解決高倍率顯微鏡檢測時的微小震動干擾，有效提升客戶設備產出率，主要應用於光
學引擎測試與系統級檢測，優勢在於可提供高精密、輕薄且體積緊湊的六軸定位平台，E1 控制器能將伺服穩定度提升
至正負 1 奈米，由於大銀的線性馬達是完全自行研發，因此可做到比業界體積更小，且可做到機電整合；大銀微開發
之多自由度奈米定位平台已切入台系與美系矽光子檢測及封裝設備鏈，2Q26 起開始交貨國內矽光子檢測設備大廠，
2H26 進⼀步放量，訂單能見度已達 2H27。 
2027 年資本支出為 1.2~1.5 億元，將新增 20~30%產能：面對全球半導體供應鏈區域化（美、日、歐晶圓廠擴建）
以及 AI 伺服器帶動高階 PCB/載板廠大舉移往泰國與東南亞設廠，大銀微採取「核心奈米平台留台灣台中擴產、標準
控制元件與次系統模組強化亞洲及歐美前線組裝」的雙軌策略，2H26 台中廠區針對高階微奈米定位系統與精密運動元
件已啟動擴產計畫，擴產產能規劃於 4Q26~1Q27 陸續完成，屆時將增加 20~30%的產能，進⼀步⽀撐 4Q26~1Q27
的出貨順暢度；第⼀階段產能擴建已完成，第二階段擴產預計於 2027 年 2 月底至 3 月中旬完成，並已開始規劃第三
階段，2027 年資本⽀出為 1.2~1.5 億元。 
微奈米定位系統營收佔比逐步攀⾼

資料來源 : 大銀微，玉山投顧整理及預估、繪製

| 大銀微的定位與優勢 
大銀微掌握直驅馬達、線性馬達與驅動控制技術：大銀微系統成立於1997年，為上銀集團旗下的機電與超精密定位核心
企業，在集團分工體系中，⺟公司上銀（2049）負責提供高剛性機械結構與精密線性傳動部件，而大銀微則負責提供「大
腦（控制器）」、「神經（驅動器）」與「直接驅動心臟（線性馬達、力矩馬達、音圈馬達）」，大銀微與上銀為機電
整合雙引擎，大銀微掌握直驅馬達、線性馬達與驅動控制技術，而上銀專精於奈米與微米級的機械傳動（螺桿、線軌、
減速機）；大銀微的優勢包括馬達與驅動器與控制器「三合⼀」全自製能力(可針對奈米級平台進行底層演算法調校)、

### Page 4

4 
集團垂直整合綜效(結合上銀高精度螺桿/滑軌與自⾝線性馬達，提供⼀站式機電整合⽅案)、已通過全球前三大半導體設
備商及晶圓代工龍頭嚴苛認證。 
產業現狀：日本政府禁止高階工具機出口中國大陸，而中國大陸也管制高精度工具機出口，在全球貿易壁壘高築下，包
括北美、東南亞訂單大舉湧向台灣工具機業者；惟台灣工具機整機廠高階市場現面臨日本工具機廠挾日圓貶值優勢的威
脅，中低階或泛用型標準機又遭逢大陸業者低價搶市的威脅。 
產品應用範圍涵蓋半導體設備、面板及光電與自動化及PCB與載板設備等：大銀微的產品應用範圍涵蓋半導體設備(佔比
逾40%)、面板及光電與自動化（佔比約30~35%）、PCB與載板設備（佔比約20%）、精密量測及自動化、生技醫療等
產業，重量級客戶有應材（AMAT）、艾斯摩爾（ASML）、科磊（KLAC）及東京威力科創(8035.T)等全球前四大半導
體設備製造廠。

| 機器人產業簡介 
服務型機器人佔比較⾼，人形機器人正在起步：依據Precedence Research預估，服務型機器人市場2026年為725億美
元，2035年將可增加至2,338億美元，年均複合成⻑率為14%；工業型機器人市場2026年為307億美元，2035年將可增
加至933億美元，年均複合成⻑率為13%；人形機器人市場2026年為22億美元，2035年將可增加至88億美元，年均複
合成⻑率為35%，人形機器人雖然市場成⻑速度較快，但由於正在起步，市場規模及滲透率仍較低。 
機器人市場

資料來源 : Precedence Research、 玉山投顧整理、繪製 
人形機器人的結構與零組件

資料來源 : IEK、 玉山投顧整理、繪製

### Page 5

5 
AI模型讓機器人可持續學習優化：機器人智能可分為三個決策層、執行層及感知層三個主要層級，決策層負責環境理解、
任務規劃與行為決策；執行層由機器人本體與運動控制系統組成，包括伺服馬達、減速機、關節模組與控制器，負責把 
AI 的決策轉換成具體動作；感知層負責蒐集環境資訊，常見設備有RGB或RGB-D攝影機、雷射測距儀、力矩與觸覺感
測器，以及慣性量測單元，透過多模態感測資訊融合，系統才能對環境與物件有更精準的理解；AI模型讓機器人可持續
學習優化，隨著人形機器人與雙臂操作機器人快速發展，機構設計也愈來愈接近人類，如多自由度關節、靈巧⼿等設計，
讓機器人能在既有的人類工作環境中執行更複雜的任務。 
機器人智能系統主要層級包括決策層、執行層與感知層

資料來源 : 台達電 
控制器相當於機器人的大腦，AI時代更形重要：控制器的主要功能包括運動控制(計算多軸反向運動學，實現精準定位與
軌跡追蹤)、信號傳遞(將邏輯決策轉為電氣信號傳給驅動器與馬達)、安全防護(緊急停止與安全監控保護人員與設備)與
系統整合(連接視覺、力覺感測器以及工廠聯網通訊) ，AI時代將更形重要。 
AI時代控制器更形重要

資料來源 : 新代法說會簡報 
人機協作為目前工廠新典型，未來將走向智慧化：人機協作是指人類與人工智慧、機器人或自動化系統共同工作、互補
優勢以達成任務的模式，它透過機器處理大量數據與重複性工作，結合人類的創造力、同理心與終端決策，實現效率與
價值的最大化；核心優勢與分工在人機協作的模式下，人類與機器各司其職，發揮最強效能，機器擅⻑資料快速運算、
例行程序自動化、預測分析與24小時監控，而人類擅⻑戰略⽅向決策、複雜情境判斷、創意發想以及人際同理心溝通；
人機協作的共同效益為大幅降低人力閒置、提升組織生產力，並協助員工從低技術工作轉型為高附加價值工作，人機協
作為目前工廠新典型，但未來將走向智慧化，智慧化機器人結合了AI、先進感測器與精密控制系統，能自主感知周遭環
境、進行即時資料分析、做出決策，並執行原本需要人類智慧與物理動作才能完成的複雜任務，將AI實體化，其核心技

### Page 6

6 
術與特點在於將大腦演算法與實體機械結合，讓機器人具備空間理解與動態適應能力，並具有高精度感測與視覺，可透
過3D深度相機、光達與扭力感測器，做到精準避障與靈敏控制，由於具有模組化關節與驅動，透過高度整合馬達、驅動
器與通訊介面，提升扭力密度與即時反應速度；智慧化機器人主要應用領域智慧製造包括彈性產線、自動化組裝、設備
巡檢與物料搬運；醫療與照護⽅面如⽀援⼿術輔助、醫療物流與高齡化社會的照護陪伴；也能應用於特殊與極端環境，
如進入災害現場、地下空間或危險區域進行偵察與救援。 
協作型機器人市場2034年預估達713億美元

資料來源 : Precedence Research、 玉山投顧整理、繪製 
協作型機器人市場至2034年的年均複合成⻑率為35%：協作型機器人（Cobot）是⼀種專為與人類在共同工作空間內安
全協作而設計的機器人，與傳統工業機器人不同，其設計核心不是「最大化速度與力量」，而是「讓機器能夠安全地在
人的旁邊工作」，協作型機器人的概念最早在 1990 年代由學術界提出，2008年丹麥Universal Robots推出第⼀款商
業化Cobot協作型機器人後，這個品類才真正進入工業應用視野。此後十餘年間，隨著感測技術、控制演算法與製造成
本的成熟，協作型機器人快速普及，從歐美大廠擴展到亞洲製造業的中小型工廠。依據Precedence Research預估，協
作型機器人市場2025年為56億美元，2034年將可增加至713億美元，年均複合成⻑率為35%。 
機器人走向通用化、智能化、自主化

資料來源 : FAUNC、KUKA、ANFAM、世紀貿易，玉山投顧整理、繪製 
傳動元件分類與比較

## Document 232: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%)과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%)을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

## Document 256: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

Deepali Bhargava and Lynn Song

tightening cycle. Inﬂation remains above target, with both headline
and core CPI holding at 2.8% year-on-year in October, while robust
semiconductor exports continue to underpin growth. At the same

China will release October industrial proﬁts on Friday. While proﬁts

would reinforce expectations for solid economic growth in the
fourth quarter.

strengthen

price pressures remain ﬁrm and reinforce our expectations for a

## Document 308: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause, however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downside 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widely 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is 
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals”. By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutinised. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike. But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, the decision is justified by the need to further analyse risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that any rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United States). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the HICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1%-2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

Anis Bensaidani   
Economist, United States, Japan 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

## Document 312: Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-20)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-20).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
09:49 November 13 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest
reading since late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of severe economic stress.
Renewed cost-of-living and interest rate pressures were front and centre following another lift in

inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage
holders are feeling the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group falling to a record low.

### Page 2

Consumers have become noticeably more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the relevant
sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge
up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business survey showed confidence slipped further
to –15 in October as conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic. Of concern for the
outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow materially faster than selling prices, further
compressing already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter financial conditions can jolt activity. The
value of new dwelling finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly decline in the series’
history, taking approvals 25% lower year-to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined with the housing tax changes announced in May.

Owner-occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home buyers proving particularly sensitive
to the deterioration in affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum across the domestic economy,
consistent with Westpac’s expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the

consensus view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the result, but core inflation was
also marginally stronger than forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the past four
months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost
inflation through. Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace, gaining 0.3% in
October.
The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor

Waller prior to the release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and the labour market while
showing significant concern over the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the passthrough
of energy and technology input inflation. His conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the
monthly rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward supporting an increase in our policy
rate of 25 basis points at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI inflation data out

later today are similar to the past two monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent or
even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also kept the focus on inflation risks and
stated or implied the FOMC needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s updates,
pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by
September. We believe this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core inflation ex

shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-month annualised basis and the labour market is
balanced; but it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate, US households are under pressure from
the cost of living, and their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing costs. Highlighting

### Page 3

Related articles
this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and 2010. Our November Market Outlook will
provide a full update on our expectations for the US and the world when released on Westpac IQ this
afternoon.
